`src/NexusForge/ViewModels/BoardInfoViewModel.cs`:

```cs
using System.Windows.Input;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Sylnar.Models;
using Sylnar.Services;

namespace Sylnar.ViewModels;

public class BoardInfoViewModel : BaseViewModel
{
    private const string Empty = "—";
    private const string NoBoardSubtitle = "Connect the board, then press Detect board";

    private readonly BoardDetectionService _boardService;
    private readonly NativeJtagService _jtagService;
    private readonly LogService _logService;
    private readonly AsyncRelayCommand _copyDnaCommand;

    private bool _isDetecting;
    private bool _isReloading;
    private bool _isBoardDetected;
    private string _connectionStatus = "Not detected";
    private Tone _connectionTone = Tone.Neutral;
    private string _deviceName = Empty;
    private string _devicePart = "No board";
    private string _deviceSubtitle = NoBoardSubtitle;
    private string _idCode = Empty;
    private string _packageName = Empty;
    private string _cableType = Empty;
    private string _bridge = Empty;
    private string _dna = Empty;
    private string _dnaFormatted = string.Empty;
    private string _copyDnaLabel = "Copy";

    public bool IsDetecting
    {
        get => _isDetecting;
        set
        {
            if (SetProperty(ref _isDetecting, value))
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(DetectButtonText));
        }
    }

    public string DetectButtonText => IsDetecting ? "Detecting…" : "Detect board";

    public bool IsBoardDetected
    {
        get => _isBoardDetected;
        set => SetProperty(ref _isBoardDetected, value);
    }

    /// <summary>Status pill text: Not detected / Detecting… / Connected / Not found / Error.</summary>
    public string ConnectionStatus
    {
        get => _connectionStatus;
        set => SetProperty(ref _connectionStatus, value);
    }

    public Tone ConnectionTone
    {
        get => _connectionTone;
        set => SetProperty(ref _connectionTone, value);
    }

    /// <summary>Full name from detection, e.g. "XC7A75T (Artix-7)".</summary>
    public string DeviceName
    {
        get => _deviceName;
        set => SetProperty(ref _deviceName, value);
    }

    /// <summary>Headline part number, e.g. "XC7A75T".</summary>
    public string DevicePart
    {
        get => _devicePart;
        set => SetProperty(ref _devicePart, value);
    }

    /// <summary>Line under the part number, e.g. "Artix-7 · detected 14:32:06".</summary>
    public string DeviceSubtitle
    {
        get => _deviceSubtitle;
        set => SetProperty(ref _deviceSubtitle, value);
    }

    public string IdCode
    {
        get => _idCode;
        set => SetProperty(ref _idCode, value);
    }

    public string PackageName
    {
        get => _packageName;
        set => SetProperty(ref _packageName, value);
    }

    public string CableType
    {
        get => _cableType;
        set => SetProperty(ref _cableType, value);
    }

    /// <summary>BSCAN SPI bridge used for flashing this part.</summary>
    public string Bridge
    {
        get => _bridge;
        set => SetProperty(ref _bridge, value);
    }

    public string Dna
    {
        get => _dna;
        set
        {
            if (!SetProperty(ref _dna, value)) return;
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasDna));
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(DnaDisplay));
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(DnaHint));
            _copyDnaCommand.NotifyCanExecuteChanged();
        }
    }

    public string DnaFormatted
    {
        get => _dnaFormatted;
        set => SetProperty(ref _dnaFormatted, value);
    }

    public bool HasDna => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(Dna) && Dna != Empty;

    /// <summary>The DNA in groups of four ("004a 91c2 e37f 5b08"); Copy still copies the raw value.</summary>
    public string DnaDisplay
    {
        get
        {
            if (!HasDna) return "──── ──── ──── ────";
            var groups = Enumerable.Range(0, (Dna.Length + 3) / 4)
                .Select(i => Dna.Substring(i * 4, Math.Min(4, Dna.Length - i * 4)));
            return string.Join(' ', groups);
        }
    }

    public string DnaHint => HasDna || !IsBoardDetected
        ? "Send this to your firmware provider"
        : "Not read: replug USB and detect again";

    public string CopyDnaLabel
    {
        get => _copyDnaLabel;
        set => SetProperty(ref _copyDnaLabel, value);
    }

    public bool IsReloading
    {
        get => _isReloading;
        set => SetProperty(ref _isReloading, value);
    }

    private int _reloadPercentage;
    public int ReloadPercentage
    {
        get => _reloadPercentage;
        set => SetProperty(ref _reloadPercentage, value);
    }

    private string _reloadMessage = string.Empty;
    public string ReloadMessage
    {
        get => _reloadMessage;
        set => SetProperty(ref _reloadMessage, value);
    }

    public ICommand DetectBoardCommand { get; }
    public ICommand CopyDnaCommand => _copyDnaCommand;
    public ICommand HotReloadCommand { get; }

    public event EventHandler? BoardDetected;
    public event EventHandler? BoardLost;

    public BoardInfoViewModel(BoardDetectionService boardService, NativeJtagService jtagService, LogService logService)
    {
        _boardService = boardService;
        _jtagService = jtagService;
        _logService = logService;
        DetectBoardCommand = new AsyncRelayCommand(DetectBoardAsync, () => !IsDetecting && !IsReloading);
        // Concurrent so the button stays enabled while it shows "Copied".
        _copyDnaCommand = new AsyncRelayCommand(CopyDnaAsync, () => HasDna,
            AsyncRelayCommandOptions.AllowConcurrentExecutions);
        HotReloadCommand = new AsyncRelayCommand(HotReloadAsync, () => !IsDetecting && !IsReloading);
    }

    private async Task HotReloadAsync()
    {
        IsReloading = true;
        ReloadPercentage = 0;
        ReloadMessage = "Starting hot reload...";

        try
        {
            var progress = new Progress<FlashProgress>(p =>
            {
                ReloadPercentage = p.Percentage;
                ReloadMessage = p.Message;
            });

            await Task.Run(() => _jtagService.HotResetFpgaAsync(progress));
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _logService.Error($"Hot reload error: {ex.Message}");
        }
        finally
        {
            IsReloading = false;
            ReloadMessage = string.Empty;
            ReloadPercentage = 0;
        }
    }

    private async Task DetectBoardAsync()
    {
        IsDetecting = true;
        ConnectionStatus = "Detecting…";
        ConnectionTone = Tone.Info;
        DeviceSubtitle = "Reading IDCODE and DNA over JTAG…";

        try
        {
            var board = await _boardService.DetectBoardAsync();

            if (board.IsDetected)
            {
                var (part, family) = SplitDeviceName(board.DeviceName);
                IsBoardDetected = true;
                ConnectionStatus = "Connected";
                ConnectionTone = Tone.Ok;
                DeviceName = board.DeviceName;
                DevicePart = part;
                DeviceSubtitle = $"{family} · detected {board.DetectedAt:HH:mm:ss}";
                IdCode = board.IdCode;
                PackageName = board.Package;
                CableType = board.JtagCable;
                Bridge = string.IsNullOrEmpty(board.Bridge) ? "None (detect only)" : board.Bridge;
                Dna = string.IsNullOrEmpty(board.Dna) ? Empty : board.Dna;
                DnaFormatted = board.DnaFormatted;
                BoardDetected?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
            }
            else
            {
                ClearBoard("Not found", Tone.Warn, "Nothing answered on JTAG. Check power and the USB cable.");
                BoardLost?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _logService.Error($"Board detection error: {ex.Message}");
            ClearBoard("Error", Tone.Danger, "Detection failed. See the log for details.");
        }
        finally
        {
            IsDetecting = false;
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(DnaHint));
        }
    }

    private void ClearBoard(string status, Tone tone, string subtitle)
    {
        IsBoardDetected = false;
        ConnectionStatus = status;
        ConnectionTone = tone;
        DeviceName = Empty;
        DevicePart = "No board";
        DeviceSubtitle = subtitle;
        IdCode = Empty;
        PackageName = Empty;
        CableType = Empty;
        Bridge = Empty;
        Dna = Empty;
        DnaFormatted = string.Empty;
    }

    /// <summary>"XC7A75T (Artix-7)" → ("XC7A75T", "Artix-7").</summary>
    private static (string Part, string Family) SplitDeviceName(string name)
    {
        if (name.StartsWith("Unknown", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            return ("Unknown part", "Xilinx, not in the supported list");

        var open = name.IndexOf(" (", StringComparison.Ordinal);
        if (open < 0 || !name.EndsWith(')'))
            return (name, "Xilinx 7-series");
        return (name[..open], name[(open + 2)..^1]);
    }

    private async Task CopyDnaAsync()
    {
        if (!HasDna) return;
        var dnaToCopy = Dna;

        try
        {
            var desktop = Avalonia.Application.Current?.ApplicationLifetime as Avalonia.Controls.ApplicationLifetimes.IClassicDesktopStyleApplicationLifetime;
            var topLevel = Avalonia.Controls.TopLevel.GetTopLevel(desktop?.MainWindow);
            if (topLevel?.Clipboard != null)
            {
                await topLevel.Clipboard.SetTextAsync(dnaToCopy);
            }
            _logService.Info($"DNA copied to clipboard: {dnaToCopy}");

            CopyDnaLabel = "Copied";
            await Task.Delay(1500);
            CopyDnaLabel = "Copy";
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _logService.Error($"Failed to copy DNA: {ex.Message}");
        }
    }
}
```

`src/NexusForge/ViewModels/DmaTestViewModel.cs`:

```cs
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.Windows.Input;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Sylnar.Models;
using Sylnar.Services;

namespace Sylnar.ViewModels;

public class DmaTestViewModel : BaseViewModel
{
    private const int StressTest = 3;

    private static readonly string[] TestNames = { "Full", "Latency", "Throughput", "Stress" };
    private static readonly string[] SpeedLabels =
        { "Reads / sec", "Throughput", "Avg read", "Min read", "Max read", "Failed reads" };
    private static readonly string[] StressLabels =
        { "Reads / sec", "Duration", "Total reads", "Fail rate", "Max fail streak", "Failed reads" };

    private readonly DmaTestService _dmaTestService;
    private readonly LogService _logService;
    private readonly AsyncRelayCommand _runSpeedTestCommand;
    private readonly AsyncRelayCommand _generateMmapCommand;
    private readonly AsyncRelayCommand _deployToToolsCommand;

    // ── Test selection ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private int _selectedTestType;
    public int SelectedTestType
    {
        get => _selectedTestType;
        set
        {
            if (value < 0 || value >= TestNames.Length) return;
            if (SetProperty(ref _selectedTestType, value))
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsStressTestSelected));
        }
    }

    // Stress duration in minutes; shown only when Stress is selected.
    private int _stressMinutes = 5;
    public int StressMinutes { get => _stressMinutes; set => SetProperty(ref _stressMinutes, Math.Clamp(value, 1, 60)); }
    public bool IsStressTestSelected => _selectedTestType == StressTest;

    // ── Run state and results ───────────────────────────────────────────────

    private bool _isTesting;
    private bool _hasResults;
    private int _testPercentage;
    private string _testMessage = "";
    private int _runType;
    private DateTime _runFinishedAt;
    private TimeSpan _runDuration;
    private string _rating = "";
    private long _failedReads;
    private bool _runFailed;

    public bool IsTesting
    {
        get => _isTesting;
        set
        {
            if (!SetProperty(ref _isTesting, value)) return;
            _runSpeedTestCommand?.NotifyCanExecuteChanged();
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(RunButtonText));
            RaiseRunChanged();
        }
    }

    public bool HasResults
    {
        get => _hasResults;
        set
        {
            if (SetProperty(ref _hasResults, value))
                RaiseRunChanged();
        }
    }

    public int TestPercentage
    {
        get => _testPercentage;
        set
        {
            if (SetProperty(ref _testPercentage, value))
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(StatusSummary));
        }
    }

    public string TestMessage
    {
        get => _testMessage;
        set
        {
            if (SetProperty(ref _testMessage, value))
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(RunMeta));
        }
    }

    public string RunButtonText => IsTesting ? "Running…" : "Run test";

    public ObservableCollection<MetricTile> Metrics { get; } = new(SpeedLabels.Select(l => new MetricTile(l)));

    /// <summary>"LAST RUN · FULL TEST", "RUNNING · STRESS TEST" or "NO RUNS YET".</summary>
    public string RunTitle =>
        IsTesting ? $"RUNNING · {TestNames[_runType].ToUpperInvariant()} TEST"
        : HasResults ? $"LAST RUN · {TestNames[_runType].ToUpperInvariant()} TEST"
        : "NO RUNS YET";

    public string RunMeta =>
        IsTesting ? TestMessage
        : !HasResults ? "Pick a test type, then press Run test"
        : _runFailed ? $"Stopped {_runFinishedAt:HH:mm:ss} · see the log"
        : $"Completed {_runFinishedAt:HH:mm:ss} · {FormatDuration(_runDuration)}";

    public string RatingText =>
        IsTesting ? "TESTING"
        : !HasResults ? "—"
        : _runFailed ? "FAIL"
        : _rating;

    public Tone RatingTone => IsTesting || !HasResults ? Tone.Neutral
        : _runFailed ? Tone.Danger
        : _rating switch
        {
            "PERFECT" => Tone.Ok,
            "EXCELLENT" => Tone.Info,
            "GOOD" => Tone.Warn,
            "FAIL" => Tone.Danger,
            _ => Tone.Neutral
        };

    public string StatusSummary =>
        IsTesting ? $"{TestPercentage}%"
        : !HasResults ? "Not tested"
        : _runFailed ? "FAIL · test did not complete"
        : $"{(_rating == "FAIL" ? "FAIL" : "PASS")} · {_failedReads:N0} failed {(_failedReads == 1 ? "read" : "reads")}";

    // Rating scale under the tiles. Latency/throughput tests share one scale;
    // the stress test is graded by duration without failures instead.
    public bool ShowSpeedScale => !(HasResults && _runType == StressTest);
    public bool ShowStressScale => !ShowSpeedScale;
    private string ScaleRating => HasResults && !_runFailed && !IsTesting ? _rating : "";
    public bool IsScaleAcceptable => ScaleRating == "ACCEPTABLE";
    public bool IsScaleGood => ScaleRating == "GOOD";
    public bool IsScaleExcellent => ScaleRating == "EXCELLENT";
    public bool IsScalePerfect => ScaleRating == "PERFECT";

    // ── Memory map ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private bool _isGeneratingMmap;
    private string _mmapStatus = "";
    private Tone _mmapStatusTone;
    private string _mmapStateText = "";
    private string _mmapAgeText = "";
    private Tone _mmapStateTone;
    private string _generateMmapLabel = "Regenerate map";

    public bool IsGeneratingMmap
    {
        get => _isGeneratingMmap;
        set
        {
            if (!SetProperty(ref _isGeneratingMmap, value)) return;
            _generateMmapCommand?.NotifyCanExecuteChanged();
            _deployToToolsCommand?.NotifyCanExecuteChanged();
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsMmapBusy));
        }
    }

    public string MmapStatus { get => _mmapStatus; set => SetProperty(ref _mmapStatus, value); }
    public Tone MmapStatusTone { get => _mmapStatusTone; set => SetProperty(ref _mmapStatusTone, value); }

    /// <summary>"Cached" or "Not generated".</summary>
    public string MmapStateText { get => _mmapStateText; private set => SetProperty(ref _mmapStateText, value); }
    /// <summary>"· 2 h old".</summary>
    public string MmapAgeText { get => _mmapAgeText; private set => SetProperty(ref _mmapAgeText, value); }
    public Tone MmapStateTone { get => _mmapStateTone; private set => SetProperty(ref _mmapStateTone, value); }
    public string GenerateMmapLabel { get => _generateMmapLabel; private set => SetProperty(ref _generateMmapLabel, value); }

    /// <summary>Where the cached map lives, with the profile folder shortened.</summary>
    public string MmapPathText { get; } = ShortenAppData(DmaTestService.MmapCachePath);

    // Deploy to DMA tools
    private bool _isDeploying;
    private string _deployStatus = "";
    private Tone _deployStatusTone;

    public bool IsDeploying
    {
        get => _isDeploying;
        set
        {
            if (!SetProperty(ref _isDeploying, value)) return;
            _deployToToolsCommand?.NotifyCanExecuteChanged();
            _generateMmapCommand?.NotifyCanExecuteChanged();
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsMmapBusy));
        }
    }

    public string DeployStatus { get => _deployStatus; set => SetProperty(ref _deployStatus, value); }
    public Tone DeployStatusTone { get => _deployStatusTone; set => SetProperty(ref _deployStatusTone, value); }

    public bool IsMmapBusy => IsGeneratingMmap || IsDeploying;

    public void RefreshMmapCacheStatus()
    {
        var written = DmaTestService.GetMmapCacheAge();
        if (written == null)
        {
            MmapStateText = "Not generated";
            MmapAgeText = "· generate before testing";
            MmapStateTone = Tone.Warn;
            GenerateMmapLabel = "Generate map";
        }
        else
        {
            var age = DateTime.Now - written.Value;
            MmapStateText = "Cached";
            MmapAgeText = $"· {FormatAge(age)} old";
            MmapStateTone = age.TotalDays > 30 ? Tone.Warn : Tone.Ok;
            GenerateMmapLabel = "Regenerate map";
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Set by the View to handle the save dialog when mmap content is ready.</summary>
    public Func<string, Task>? MmapReadyToSave { get; set; }

    public ICommand RunSpeedTestCommand => _runSpeedTestCommand;
    public ICommand GenerateMmapCommand => _generateMmapCommand;
    public ICommand DeployToToolsCommand => _deployToToolsCommand;

    public DmaTestViewModel(DmaTestService dmaTestService, LogService logService)
    {
        _dmaTestService = dmaTestService;
        _logService = logService;

        _runSpeedTestCommand = new AsyncRelayCommand(RunSpeedTestAsync, () => !IsTesting);
        _generateMmapCommand = new AsyncRelayCommand(GenerateMmapAsync, () => !IsGeneratingMmap && !IsDeploying);
        _deployToToolsCommand = new AsyncRelayCommand(DeployToToolsAsync, () => !IsDeploying && !IsGeneratingMmap);

        RefreshMmapCacheStatus();
    }

    private async Task RunSpeedTestAsync()
    {
        _runType = SelectedTestType;
        _runFailed = false;
        _rating = "";
        ResetMetrics();
        TestPercentage = 0;
        TestMessage = "Starting...";
        IsTesting = true;

        var started = DateTime.Now;
        try
        {
            var progress = new Progress<FlashProgress>(p =>
            {
                TestPercentage = p.Percentage;
                TestMessage = p.Message;
            });

            DmaTestResult result = _runType switch
            {
                1 => await _dmaTestService.RunLatencyTestAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(30), progress, CancellationToken.None),
                2 => await _dmaTestService.RunThroughputTestAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(15), progress, CancellationToken.None),
                StressTest => await _dmaTestService.RunStressTestAsync(TimeSpan.FromMinutes(StressMinutes), progress, CancellationToken.None),
                _ => await _dmaTestService.RunFullTestAsync(progress, CancellationToken.None)
            };

            ApplyResults(result);
            _runDuration = result.Duration > TimeSpan.Zero ? result.Duration : DateTime.Now - started;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _logService.Error($"Speed test error: {ex.Message}");
            _runFailed = true;
        }
        finally
        {
            _runFinishedAt = DateTime.Now;
            HasResults = true;
            IsTesting = false;
        }
    }

    private async Task GenerateMmapAsync()
    {
        IsGeneratingMmap = true;
        MmapStatus = "Connecting to DMA device...";
        MmapStatusTone = Tone.Info;

        try
        {
            var progress = new Progress<FlashProgress>(p => MmapStatus = p.Message);

            var result = await _dmaTestService.GenerateMmapAsync(progress, CancellationToken.None);

            if (result.Success)
            {
                MmapStatusTone = Tone.Ok;
                RefreshMmapCacheStatus();
                if (MmapReadyToSave != null)
                    await MmapReadyToSave(result.Content);
            }
            else
            {
                MmapStatus = result.ErrorMessage;
                MmapStatusTone = Tone.Danger;
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            MmapStatus = $"Failed: {ex.Message}";
            MmapStatusTone = Tone.Danger;
            _logService.Error($"mmap generation error: {ex.Message}");
        }
        finally
        {
            IsGeneratingMmap = false;
        }
    }

    private async Task DeployToToolsAsync()
    {
        IsDeploying = true;
        DeployStatus = "Preparing deployment...";
        DeployStatusTone = Tone.Info;

        try
        {
            // Ensure a fresh mmap exists; generate if not cached, then read content.
            string mmapContent = "";
            if (DmaTestService.HasCachedMmap())
            {
                try { mmapContent = await File.ReadAllTextAsync(DmaTestService.MmapCachePath); }
                catch { mmapContent = ""; }
            }

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(mmapContent))
            {
                DeployStatus = "No mmap cached - generating first...";
                var genProgress = new Progress<FlashProgress>(p => DeployStatus = p.Message);
                var gen = await _dmaTestService.GenerateMmapAsync(genProgress, CancellationToken.None);
                if (gen.Success)
                {
                    mmapContent = gen.Content;
                    RefreshMmapCacheStatus();
                }
                else
                {
                    DeployStatus = $"Could not generate mmap: {gen.ErrorMessage}. Deploying DLLs without mmap.";
                    mmapContent = "";
                }
            }

            var progress = new Progress<FlashProgress>(p => DeployStatus = p.Message);
            var result = await _dmaTestService.DeployToToolsAsync(mmapContent, progress, CancellationToken.None);

            if (result.Success)
            {
                DeployStatus =
                    $"{result.FoldersFound} folder(s): {result.LeechcoreReplaced} leechcore, " +
                    $"{result.FtdiChainCompleted} FTDI chain, {result.MmapWritten} mmap" +
                    (result.Skipped.Count > 0 ? $" - {result.Skipped.Count} skipped" : "") +
                    (result.Flagged.Count > 0 ? $" - {result.Flagged.Count} flagged" : "");

                DeployStatusTone = result.FoldersFound == 0 || result.Skipped.Count > 0 ? Tone.Warn : Tone.Ok;

                if (result.FoldersFound == 0)
                    DeployStatus = "No DMA tool folders found on any fixed drive.";
            }
            else
            {
                DeployStatus = result.Error;
                DeployStatusTone = Tone.Danger;
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            DeployStatus = $"Deploy failed: {ex.Message}";
            DeployStatusTone = Tone.Danger;
            _logService.Error($"Deploy to tools error: {ex.Message}");
        }
        finally
        {
            IsDeploying = false;
        }
    }

    private void ResetMetrics()
    {
        var labels = _runType == StressTest ? StressLabels : SpeedLabels;
        for (int i = 0; i < Metrics.Count; i++)
        {
            Metrics[i].Label = labels[i];
            Metrics[i].Value = "—";
        }
    }

    private void SetMetrics(params string[] values)
    {
        for (int i = 0; i < Metrics.Count && i < values.Length; i++)
            Metrics[i].Value = values[i];
    }

    private void ApplyResults(DmaTestResult result)
    {
        if (!result.Success)
        {
            _runFailed = true;
            return;
        }

        _rating = result.OverallRating;

        if (result.TestType == "Stress")
        {
            var rps = result.StressDuration.TotalSeconds > 0 ? result.StressTotalReads / result.StressDuration.TotalSeconds : 0;
            _failedReads = result.StressFailedReads;
            SetMetrics(
                $"{rps:N0}",
                $"{result.StressDuration.TotalMinutes:F1} min",
                $"{result.StressTotalReads:N0}",
                $"{result.StressFailPct:F3} %",
                $"{result.StressMaxConsecFails:N0}",
                $"{result.StressFailedReads:N0}");
            return;
        }

        _failedReads = result.LatencyFailedReads + result.ThroughputFailedReads;
        bool hasLatency = result.LatencyRps > 0;
        SetMetrics(
            hasLatency ? $"{result.LatencyRps:N0}" : "—",
            result.ThroughputMBps > 0 ? $"{result.ThroughputMBps:N0} MB/s"
                : result.ThroughputRating == "SKIP" ? "Skipped" : "—",
            hasLatency ? $"{result.LatencyAvgUs:N0} µs" : "—",
            hasLatency ? $"{result.LatencyMinUs:N0} µs" : "—",
            hasLatency ? $"{result.LatencyMaxUs:N0} µs" : "—",
            $"{_failedReads:N0}");
    }

    private void RaiseRunChanged()
    {
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(RunTitle));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(RunMeta));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(RatingText));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(RatingTone));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(StatusSummary));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ShowSpeedScale));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ShowStressScale));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsScaleAcceptable));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsScaleGood));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsScaleExcellent));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsScalePerfect));
    }

    private static string FormatDuration(TimeSpan t) =>
        t.TotalSeconds < 60 ? $"{Math.Max(1, (int)Math.Round(t.TotalSeconds))} s"
        : t.TotalMinutes < 60 ? $"{(int)t.TotalMinutes} min {t.Seconds} s"
        : $"{(int)t.TotalHours} h {t.Minutes} min";

    private static string FormatAge(TimeSpan age) =>
        age.TotalMinutes < 1 ? "under a minute"
        : age.TotalHours < 1 ? $"{(int)age.TotalMinutes} min"
        : age.TotalDays < 1 ? $"{(int)age.TotalHours} h"
        : age.TotalDays < 2 ? "1 day"
        : $"{(int)age.TotalDays} days";

    private static string ShortenAppData(string path)
    {
        var appData = Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.ApplicationData);
        return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(appData) && path.StartsWith(appData, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
            ? "%AppData%" + path[appData.Length..]
            : path;
    }
}
```

`src/NexusForge/ViewModels/DriverStatusViewModel.cs`:

```cs
using System.Windows.Input;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Sylnar.Models;
using Sylnar.Services;

namespace Sylnar.ViewModels;

/// <summary>Per-driver wording for a <see cref="DriverStatusViewModel"/> card.</summary>
public sealed record DriverCopy(
    string Title,
    string Subtitle,
    string ShortName,
    string BundledDriver,
    string ReadyMessage,
    string MissingMessage,
    string NotConnectedMessage,
    string WrongDriverMessage = "");

/// <summary>
/// One USB link's driver (CH347 JTAG or FT601 data): its last check, the
/// Check / Install / Uninstall actions, and how the card and sidebar show it.
/// </summary>
public sealed class DriverStatusViewModel : BaseViewModel
{
    private enum State { NotChecked, Installed, Missing, WrongDriver, NotConnected, CheckFailed }

    private const string Empty = "—";

    private readonly DriverCopy _copy;
    private readonly Func<Task<DriverInfo>> _check;
    private readonly Func<Task<bool>> _install;
    private readonly Func<string, Task<bool>> _uninstall;
    private readonly LogService _log;
    private readonly AsyncRelayCommand _checkCommand;
    private readonly AsyncRelayCommand _installCommand;
    private readonly AsyncRelayCommand _uninstallCommand;

    private State _state = State.NotChecked;
    private bool _isBusy;
    private string _busyMessage = string.Empty;
    private string _busyPill = string.Empty;
    private string? _actionNote;
    private string _deviceName = Empty;
    private string _version = Empty;
    private string _driverType = Empty;
    private string _vidPid;
    private string _infPath = string.Empty;

    public DriverStatusViewModel(
        DriverCopy copy,
        string vidPid,
        Func<Task<DriverInfo>> check,
        Func<Task<bool>> install,
        Func<string, Task<bool>> uninstall,
        LogService log)
    {
        _copy = copy;
        _vidPid = vidPid;
        _check = check;
        _install = install;
        _uninstall = uninstall;
        _log = log;

        _checkCommand = new AsyncRelayCommand(CheckAsync, () => !IsBusy);
        _installCommand = new AsyncRelayCommand(InstallAsync, () => !IsBusy);
        // Nothing to remove when the device is present without a driver.
        _uninstallCommand = new AsyncRelayCommand(UninstallAsync,
            () => !IsBusy && _state is not (State.NotChecked or State.Missing));
    }

    public string Title => _copy.Title;
    public string Subtitle => _copy.Subtitle;
    public string ShortName => _copy.ShortName;

    public ICommand CheckCommand => _checkCommand;
    public ICommand InstallCommand => _installCommand;
    public ICommand UninstallCommand => _uninstallCommand;

    public bool IsBusy
    {
        get => _isBusy;
        private set
        {
            if (!SetProperty(ref _isBusy, value)) return;
            _checkCommand.NotifyCanExecuteChanged();
            _installCommand.NotifyCanExecuteChanged();
            _uninstallCommand.NotifyCanExecuteChanged();
            RaiseStatusChanged();
        }
    }

    public bool IsDriverOk => _state == State.Installed;

    // ── Card ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public string StatusText => IsBusy ? _busyPill : _state switch
    {
        State.Installed => "Installed",
        State.Missing => "Missing",
        State.WrongDriver => "Old driver",
        State.NotConnected => "Not connected",
        State.CheckFailed => "Check failed",
        _ => "Not checked"
    };

    public Tone StatusTone => IsBusy ? Tone.Info : _state switch
    {
        State.Installed => Tone.Ok,
        State.Missing or State.WrongDriver => Tone.Warn,
        State.CheckFailed => Tone.Danger,
        _ => Tone.Neutral
    };

    /// <summary>Card border and icon tile colour.</summary>
    public Tone CardTone => _state switch
    {
        State.Installed => Tone.Ok,
        State.Missing or State.WrongDriver => Tone.Warn,
        State.CheckFailed => Tone.Danger,
        _ => Tone.Neutral
    };

    public string Message => IsBusy ? _busyMessage : _actionNote ?? _state switch
    {
        State.Installed => _copy.ReadyMessage,
        State.Missing => _copy.MissingMessage,
        State.WrongDriver => _copy.WrongDriverMessage,
        State.NotConnected => _copy.NotConnectedMessage,
        State.CheckFailed => "The driver check did not finish. See the log for details, then try again.",
        _ => "Not checked yet. Press Check to read the driver status."
    };

    public Tone MessageTone => IsBusy ? Tone.Info : _actionNote != null ? Tone.Warn : _state switch
    {
        State.Installed => Tone.Ok,
        State.Missing or State.WrongDriver => Tone.Warn,
        State.CheckFailed => Tone.Danger,
        _ => Tone.Neutral
    };

    public string DeviceText => _state switch
    {
        State.Missing => $"{_deviceName} (no driver)",
        State.NotChecked or State.NotConnected or State.CheckFailed => Empty,
        _ => _deviceName
    };

    public string VidPidText => _vidPid.Replace(":", " : ");

    public string VersionText => _state is State.Installed or State.WrongDriver ? _version : Empty;

    public bool HasVersion => VersionText != Empty;

    /// <summary>The bound driver, or the bundled package Install will use.</summary>
    public string DriverText => (_state is State.Installed or State.WrongDriver) && _driverType != Empty
        ? _driverType
        : _copy.BundledDriver;

    // ── Sidebar ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public string SidebarText => IsBusy ? _busyPill : _state switch
    {
        State.Installed => "Ready",
        State.Missing => "Driver missing",
        State.WrongDriver => "Old driver",
        State.NotConnected => "Not connected",
        State.CheckFailed => "Check failed",
        _ => "Not checked"
    };

    public Tone SidebarTone => IsBusy ? Tone.Neutral : StatusTone;

    // ── Actions ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public async Task CheckAsync()
    {
        if (IsBusy) return;
        SetBusy("Checking…", "Checking driver status…");
        try
        {
            await CheckCoreAsync();
        }
        finally
        {
            IsBusy = false;
        }
    }

    private async Task CheckCoreAsync()
    {
        _actionNote = null;
        try
        {
            var info = await _check();

            _deviceName = info.DeviceName;
            _version = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(info.Version) ? Empty : info.Version;
            _driverType = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(info.DriverType) ? Empty : info.DriverType;
            _vidPid = info.VidPid;
            _infPath = info.InfPath;

            _state = info.Status == "Check failed" ? State.CheckFailed
                : !info.IsDeviceDetected ? State.NotConnected
                : info.IsDriverOk ? State.Installed
                : info.Status == "Wrong Driver" ? State.WrongDriver
                : State.Missing;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _log.Error($"{ShortName} driver check failed: {ex.Message}");
            _state = State.CheckFailed;
        }
        _uninstallCommand.NotifyCanExecuteChanged();
        RaiseStatusChanged();
    }

    private async Task InstallAsync()
    {
        SetBusy("Installing…", "Installing driver (administrator rights needed)…");
        try
        {
            bool ok = await _install();

            _busyMessage = "Verifying installation…";
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(Message));
            if (ok) await Task.Delay(1500);
            await CheckCoreAsync();

            // Not bound yet: usually a replug or, if Windows asked, a restart.
            if (!ok && _state != State.Installed)
                _actionNote = "The driver was added but is not active yet. Replug the USB cable and press Check; restart first if Windows asked for it.";
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _log.Error($"{ShortName} driver install failed: {ex.Message}");
            _actionNote = "The install did not finish. See the log for the reason.";
        }
        finally
        {
            IsBusy = false;
        }
    }

    private async Task UninstallAsync()
    {
        SetBusy("Removing…", "Removing driver (administrator rights needed)…");
        try
        {
            bool ok = await _uninstall(_infPath);
            if (ok)
            {
                _busyMessage = "Verifying removal…";
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(Message));
                await Task.Delay(1500);
            }
            await CheckCoreAsync();
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _log.Error($"{ShortName} driver removal failed: {ex.Message}");
        }
        finally
        {
            IsBusy = false;
        }
    }

    private void SetBusy(string pill, string message)
    {
        _busyPill = pill;
        _busyMessage = message;
        IsBusy = true;
    }

    private void RaiseStatusChanged()
    {
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsDriverOk));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(StatusText));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(StatusTone));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CardTone));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(Message));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(MessageTone));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(DeviceText));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(VidPidText));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(VersionText));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasVersion));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(DriverText));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(SidebarText));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(SidebarTone));
    }
}
```

```cs
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Windows.Input;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Sylnar.Services;

namespace Sylnar.ViewModels;

/// <summary>The Drivers page: both USB links side by side.</summary>
public class DriverViewModel : BaseViewModel
{
    private readonly AsyncRelayCommand _checkBothCommand;

    /// <summary>CH347 USB-JTAG: detection, DNA and flashing.</summary>
    public DriverStatusViewModel Ch347 { get; }

    /// <summary>FTDI FT601 USB 3 data port: DMA transfers and speed tests.</summary>
    public DriverStatusViewModel Ft601 { get; }

    public ICommand CheckBothCommand => _checkBothCommand;

    public DriverViewModel(DriverService driverService, FtdiDriverService ftdiService, LogService logService)
    {
        Ch347 = new DriverStatusViewModel(
            new DriverCopy(
                Title: "CH347 · JTAG",
                Subtitle: "Detection, DNA and flashing",
                ShortName: "CH347 JTAG",
                BundledDriver: "CH341WDM (bundled, signed)",
                ReadyMessage: "Driver active. JTAG is ready for detection and flashing.",
                MissingMessage: "The JTAG cable is connected but has no driver. Install it, then replug the USB cable.",
                NotConnectedMessage: "No CH347 found. Plug in the JTAG USB cable (the smaller port on the board), then press Check."),
            "1A86:55DD",
            driverService.CheckDriverAsync,
            driverService.InstallDriverAsync,
            driverService.UninstallDriverAsync,
            logService);

        Ft601 = new DriverStatusViewModel(
            new DriverCopy(
                Title: "FT601 · USB 3 data",
                Subtitle: "DMA transfers and speed tests",
                ShortName: "FT601 Data",
                BundledDriver: "FTD3XXWU (bundled)",
                ReadyMessage: "Driver active. The DMA data path is ready.",
                MissingMessage: "The data cable is connected but has no driver. Install it, then replug the USB 3 cable.",
                NotConnectedMessage: "No FT601 found. Connect the USB 3 data cable, then press Check.",
                WrongDriverMessage: "An old FTDI driver is bound. LeechCore needs the WinUSB SuperSpeed driver: install to replace it."),
            "0403:601F",
            ftdiService.CheckDriverAsync,
            ftdiService.InstallDriverAsync,
            ftdiService.UninstallDriverAsync,
            logService);

        _checkBothCommand = new AsyncRelayCommand(CheckBothAsync, () => !Ch347.IsBusy && !Ft601.IsBusy);
        Ch347.PropertyChanged += OnDriverChanged;
        Ft601.PropertyChanged += OnDriverChanged;
    }

    public Task CheckBothAsync() => Task.WhenAll(Ch347.CheckAsync(), Ft601.CheckAsync());

    private void OnDriverChanged(object? sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (e.PropertyName == nameof(DriverStatusViewModel.IsBusy))
            _checkBothCommand.NotifyCanExecuteChanged();
    }
}
```

`src/NexusForge/ViewModels/FlashStep.cs`:

```cs
namespace Sylnar.ViewModels;

/// <summary>One segment of the flash step bar (Bridge · Erase · Write · Done).</summary>
public sealed class FlashStep : BaseViewModel
{
    private double _progress;
    private bool _isActive;
    private bool _isDone;
    private bool _isFailed;

    public FlashStep(string label) => Label = label;

    public string Label { get; }

    /// <summary>Fill of this step's bar, 0..1.</summary>
    public double Progress
    {
        get => _progress;
        private set => SetProperty(ref _progress, value);
    }

    public bool IsActive
    {
        get => _isActive;
        private set => SetProperty(ref _isActive, value);
    }

    public bool IsDone
    {
        get => _isDone;
        private set => SetProperty(ref _isDone, value);
    }

    public bool IsFailed
    {
        get => _isFailed;
        private set => SetProperty(ref _isFailed, value);
    }

    public void SetPending() => Set(0, active: false, done: false, failed: false);

    public void SetActive(double fraction) => Set(Math.Clamp(fraction, 0, 1), active: true, done: false, failed: false);

    public void SetDone() => Set(1, active: false, done: true, failed: false);

    public void SetFailed() => Set(Math.Max(Progress, 0.15), active: true, done: false, failed: true);

    private void Set(double progress, bool active, bool done, bool failed)
    {
        Progress = progress;
        IsActive = active;
        IsDone = done;
        IsFailed = failed;
    }
}
```

`src/NexusForge/ViewModels/FlashViewModel.cs`:

```cs
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Windows.Input;
using Avalonia.Threading;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Sylnar.Helpers;
using Sylnar.Models;
using Sylnar.Services;

namespace Sylnar.ViewModels;

public class FlashViewModel : BaseViewModel
{
    private enum RunState { Idle, Running, Succeeded, Failed, Cancelled }

    private static readonly string[] SpiSteps = { "Bridge", "Erase", "Write", "Done" };
    private static readonly string[] SramSteps = { "Connect", "Program", "Done" };

    // Stages reported before the programmer process starts. Cancel is honoured
    // only there: after that, stopping would leave a half-written flash.
    private static readonly HashSet<string> CancellableStages =
        new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase) { "Starting...", "Initializing", "Detecting" };

    private readonly FlashService _flashService;
    private readonly LogService _logService;
    private readonly AppSettings _settings;
    private readonly AsyncRelayCommand _flashFirmwareCommand;
    private readonly RelayCommand _cancelFlashCommand;
    private readonly DispatcherTimer _elapsedTimer;
    private readonly Stopwatch _stopwatch = new();

    private string _firmwarePath = string.Empty;
    private string _firmwareFileName = "No file selected";
    private string _fileSizeText = "—";
    private bool _isFileValid;
    private string _fileValidationMessage = string.Empty;
    private int _flashPercentage;
    private string _flashStage = string.Empty;
    private string _flashMessage = string.Empty;
    private bool _isFlashing;
    private bool _isFlashComplete;
    private bool _hasFlashError;
    private string _flashErrorMessage = string.Empty;
    private CancellationTokenSource? _cts;
    private RunState _runState = RunState.Idle;
    private int _activeStep;
    private int _furthestStep;
    private int _furthestStage;

    /// <summary>
    /// How far through a flash a stage is. Used to drop reports that arrive out
    /// of order; everything before the erase shares rank 0, since those are all
    /// the opening step and their order between themselves does not matter.
    /// </summary>
    private static int StageRank(string stage) => stage switch
    {
        "Erasing" => 1,
        "Erased" => 2,
        "Writing" or "Programming" => 3,
        "Written" => 4,
        "Complete" => 5,
        _ => 0,
    };

    public ObservableCollection<FlashStep> Steps { get; } = new();

    public string FirmwarePath
    {
        get => _firmwarePath;
        set
        {
            if (SetProperty(ref _firmwarePath, value))
            {
                // A new file makes the previous run's result stale.
                IsFlashComplete = false;
                HasFlashError = false;
                SetRunState(RunState.Idle);
                ValidateFile();
                ResetSteps();
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasFile));
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsBitFile));
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(FlashModeText));
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CancelNote));
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(FileDetailText));
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(FileDetailTone));
            }
        }
    }

    public string FirmwareFileName
    {
        get => _firmwareFileName;
        set => SetProperty(ref _firmwareFileName, value);
    }

    public string FileSizeText
    {
        get => _fileSizeText;
        set => SetProperty(ref _fileSizeText, value);
    }

    public bool IsFileValid
    {
        get => _isFileValid;
        set
        {
            if (SetProperty(ref _isFileValid, value))
            {
                _flashFirmwareCommand?.NotifyCanExecuteChanged();
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ProgressText));
            }
        }
    }

    public string FileValidationMessage
    {
        get => _fileValidationMessage;
        set => SetProperty(ref _fileValidationMessage, value);
    }

    public bool HasFile => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(FirmwarePath);

    public bool IsBitFile => FirmwarePath.EndsWith(".bit", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

    public string FlashModeText => IsBitFile ? "SRAM · until power-off" : "SPI flash · persistent";

    public string FileDetailText =>
        !HasFile ? "Drop a .bin or .bit file here, or browse"
        : IsFileValid ? $"{FileSizeText} · drop a .bin or .bit here, or browse"
        : FileValidationMessage;

    public Tone FileDetailTone => HasFile && !IsFileValid ? Tone.Warn : Tone.Neutral;

    public string CancelNote => IsBitFile
        ? "Cancel works until the bitstream starts loading. A .bit load is lost at power-off anyway."
        : "Cancel works until the SPI erase starts — stopping mid-write would leave the board unbootable.";

    public int FlashPercentage
    {
        get => _flashPercentage;
        set
        {
            if (SetProperty(ref _flashPercentage, value))
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(PercentText));
        }
    }

    public string PercentText => $"{FlashPercentage}%";

    public string FlashStage
    {
        get => _flashStage;
        set
        {
            if (SetProperty(ref _flashStage, value))
            {
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CanCancel));
                _cancelFlashCommand?.NotifyCanExecuteChanged();
            }
        }
    }

    public string FlashMessage
    {
        get => _flashMessage;
        set
        {
            if (SetProperty(ref _flashMessage, value))
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ProgressText));
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Status line above the bar: the live stage message while flashing.</summary>
    public string ProgressText => _runState switch
    {
        RunState.Running => FlashMessage,
        RunState.Succeeded => "Flash complete",
        RunState.Failed => "Flash failed",
        RunState.Cancelled => "Flash cancelled",
        _ => !HasFile ? "No firmware selected" : IsFileValid ? "Ready to flash" : "This file can't be flashed"
    };

    public Tone ProgressTone => _runState switch
    {
        RunState.Running or RunState.Succeeded => Tone.Ok,
        RunState.Failed => Tone.Danger,
        _ => Tone.Neutral
    };

    public bool IsFailedRun => _runState == RunState.Failed;

    public string ElapsedText => _runState switch
    {
        RunState.Running => $"Elapsed {FormatElapsed(_stopwatch.Elapsed)}",
        RunState.Succeeded => $"Finished in {FormatElapsed(_stopwatch.Elapsed)}",
        RunState.Failed or RunState.Cancelled => $"Stopped after {FormatElapsed(_stopwatch.Elapsed)}",
        _ => ".bin writes SPI flash (kept at power-off) · .bit loads SRAM (lost at power-off)"
    };

    public bool IsFlashing
    {
        get => _isFlashing;
        set
        {
            if (SetProperty(ref _isFlashing, value))
            {
                _flashFirmwareCommand?.NotifyCanExecuteChanged();
                _cancelFlashCommand?.NotifyCanExecuteChanged();
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CanCancel));
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(FlashButtonText));
            }
        }
    }

    public string FlashButtonText => IsFlashing ? "Flashing…" : "Flash firmware";

    public bool CanCancel => IsFlashing && CancellableStages.Contains(FlashStage);

    public bool IsFlashComplete
    {
        get => _isFlashComplete;
        set => SetProperty(ref _isFlashComplete, value);
    }

    public bool HasFlashError
    {
        get => _hasFlashError;
        set => SetProperty(ref _hasFlashError, value);
    }

    /// <summary>What to do after a successful flash; differs for SPI (.bin) vs SRAM (.bit).</summary>
    public string FlashSuccessMessage =>
        IsBitFile
            ? "Bitstream loaded into the FPGA. It is active now and is lost on power-off."
            : "Firmware written to SPI flash. Fully power-cycle the target PC to load it.";

    public string FlashErrorMessage
    {
        get => _flashErrorMessage;
        set => SetProperty(ref _flashErrorMessage, value);
    }

    public ICommand BrowseFileCommand { get; }
    public ICommand FlashFirmwareCommand => _flashFirmwareCommand;
    public ICommand CancelFlashCommand => _cancelFlashCommand;

    public FlashViewModel(FlashService flashService, LogService logService, AppSettings settings)
    {
        _flashService = flashService;
        _logService = logService;
        _settings = settings;
        BrowseFileCommand = new AsyncRelayCommand(BrowseFileAsync);
        _flashFirmwareCommand = new AsyncRelayCommand(FlashFirmwareAsync, () => IsFileValid && !IsFlashing);
        _cancelFlashCommand = new RelayCommand(CancelFlash, () => CanCancel);

        _elapsedTimer = new DispatcherTimer { Interval = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1) };
        _elapsedTimer.Tick += (_, _) => OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ElapsedText));

        ResetSteps();

        // Restore the firmware picked last session so a re-flash is one click.
        var last = _settings.LastFirmwarePath;
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(last) && File.Exists(last))
            FirmwarePath = last;
    }

    /// <summary>A file dropped on the Firmware card.</summary>
    public void LoadDroppedFile(string path)
    {
        if (IsFlashing) return;
        FirmwarePath = path;
    }

    private async Task BrowseFileAsync()
    {
        try
        {
            var desktop = Avalonia.Application.Current?.ApplicationLifetime as Avalonia.Controls.ApplicationLifetimes.IClassicDesktopStyleApplicationLifetime;
            var topLevel = Avalonia.Controls.TopLevel.GetTopLevel(desktop?.MainWindow);

            if (topLevel?.StorageProvider is { } sp)
            {
                var files = await sp.OpenFilePickerAsync(new Avalonia.Platform.Storage.FilePickerOpenOptions
                {
                    Title = "Select Firmware File",
                    AllowMultiple = false,
                    FileTypeFilter = new List<Avalonia.Platform.Storage.FilePickerFileType>
                    {
                        new("Firmware Files") { Patterns = new List<string> { "*.bin", "*.bit" } },
                        new("All Files") { Patterns = new List<string> { "*.*" } }
                    }
                });

                if (files != null && files.Count > 0)
                {
                    var uri = files[0].Path;
                    if (uri.IsFile)
                        FirmwarePath = uri.LocalPath;
                }
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _logService.Error($"File browser error: {ex.Message}");
        }
    }

    private void ValidateFile()
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(FirmwarePath))
        {
            FirmwareFileName = "No file selected";
            FileSizeText = "—";
            IsFileValid = false;
            return;
        }

        var firmware = FileValidator.Validate(FirmwarePath);
        FirmwareFileName = firmware.FileName;
        FileSizeText = firmware.FileSize > 0 ? $"{firmware.FileSize / 1024:N0} KB" : "—";
        IsFileValid = firmware.IsValid;
        FileValidationMessage = firmware.ValidationMessage;
        if (firmware.IsValid)
            _settings.LastFirmwarePath = FirmwarePath;
    }

    private async Task FlashFirmwareAsync()
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(FirmwarePath) || !IsFileValid)
            return;

        IsFlashComplete = false;
        HasFlashError = false;
        FlashPercentage = 0;
        FlashStage = "Starting...";
        FlashMessage = "Preparing to flash firmware...";
        IsFlashing = true;
        ResetSteps();
        SetActiveStep(0, 0);
        _stopwatch.Restart();
        _elapsedTimer.Start();
        SetRunState(RunState.Running);

        _cts = new CancellationTokenSource();

        var progress = new Progress<FlashProgress>(p =>
        {
            // A report can be queued behind the final result; ignore it then.
            if (_runState != RunState.Running) return;

            // "Failed" reports 0%; keep the bar where it stopped.
            if (p.Stage == "Failed")
            {
                FlashStage = p.Stage;
                FlashMessage = p.Message;
                MarkActiveStepFailed();
            }
            else
            {
                // Drop a report that belongs to a stage already passed. The
                // service reads OpenOCD's stderr and stdout on two independent
                // tasks with no ordering between them, so an erase line can be
                // handled after a write line from the other pipe; applying it
                // would wind the percentage and the message backwards while the
                // flash is in fact further along.
                int rank = StageRank(p.Stage);
                if (rank < _furthestStage) return;
                _furthestStage = rank;

                FlashStage = p.Stage;
                FlashMessage = p.Message;
                FlashPercentage = p.Percentage;
                ApplyStageToSteps(p.Stage, p.Percentage);
            }

            if (p.IsComplete)
                IsFlashComplete = true;
            if (p.HasError)
            {
                HasFlashError = true;
                FlashErrorMessage = p.ErrorMessage;
            }
        });

        try
        {
            var result = await _flashService.FlashAsync(FirmwarePath, progress, _cts.Token);

            if (result.Success)
            {
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(FlashSuccessMessage));
                IsFlashComplete = true;
                FlashPercentage = 100;
                FlashStage = "Complete";
                FlashMessage = "Firmware flashed successfully!";
                SetActiveStep(Steps.Count, 0);
                SetRunState(RunState.Succeeded);
            }
            else
            {
                HasFlashError = true;
                FlashErrorMessage = result.ErrorMessage;
                FlashStage = "Failed";
                FlashMessage = $"Flash failed: {result.ErrorMessage}";
                MarkActiveStepFailed();
                SetRunState(RunState.Failed);
            }
        }
        catch (OperationCanceledException)
        {
            FlashStage = "Cancelled";
            FlashMessage = "Flash operation was cancelled";
            if (_activeStep < Steps.Count) Steps[_activeStep].SetPending();
            SetRunState(RunState.Cancelled);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            HasFlashError = true;
            FlashErrorMessage = ex.Message;
            FlashStage = "Error";
            FlashMessage = $"Flash error: {ex.Message}";
            MarkActiveStepFailed();
            SetRunState(RunState.Failed);
        }
        finally
        {
            _stopwatch.Stop();
            _elapsedTimer.Stop();
            IsFlashing = false;
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ElapsedText));
            _cts?.Dispose();
            _cts = null;
        }
    }

    private void CancelFlash()
    {
        // Honoured only before the SPI erase/write starts; after that the
        // service logs that the write will be allowed to finish.
        _logService.Warn("Cancel requested by user.");
        _cts?.Cancel();
    }

    private void SetRunState(RunState state)
    {
        _runState = state;
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ProgressText));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ProgressTone));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsFailedRun));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ElapsedText));
    }

    private void ResetSteps()
    {
        Steps.Clear();
        foreach (var label in IsBitFile ? SramSteps : SpiSteps)
            Steps.Add(new FlashStep(label));
        _activeStep = 0;
        _furthestStep = 0;
        _furthestStage = 0;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Maps the service's stage/percentage onto the step bar.
    ///
    /// Only ever forwards. The service parses two pipes - OpenOCD's log goes to
    /// stderr while the write's per-page lines are a plain printf on stdout - and
    /// they are drained by two independent tasks, so a line can be handled after
    /// a later one from the other pipe. Without this the bar could be dragged
    /// back from Write to Erase by a straggling erase line. Flashing only ever
    /// moves forward, so the furthest step reached is the honest one to show.
    /// </summary>
    private void ApplyStageToSteps(string stage, int pct)
    {
        var (index, fraction) = IsBitFile
            ? stage switch
            {
                "Programming" => (1, 0.5),
                "Complete" => (Steps.Count, 0.0),
                _ => (0, pct / 20.0)
            }
            : stage switch
            {
                "Erasing" => (1, (pct - 15) / 40.0),
                "Erased" => (2, 0.0),
                "Writing" => (2, (pct - 55) / 33.0),
                "Written" => (3, 0.5),
                "Complete" => (Steps.Count, 0.0),
                _ => (0, pct / 15.0)
            };

        if (index < _furthestStep)
        {
            // A straggler from the other pipe. Hold the step it belongs to at
            // full, rather than un-doing the progress already shown.
            if (index >= 0 && index < Steps.Count) Steps[index].SetDone();
            return;
        }

        _furthestStep = index;
        SetActiveStep(index, fraction);
    }

    private void SetActiveStep(int index, double fraction)
    {
        _activeStep = index;
        if (index > _furthestStep) _furthestStep = index;
        for (int i = 0; i < Steps.Count; i++)
        {
            if (i < index) Steps[i].SetDone();
            else if (i == index) Steps[i].SetActive(fraction);
            else Steps[i].SetPending();
        }
    }

    private void MarkActiveStepFailed()
    {
        if (_activeStep < Steps.Count)
            Steps[_activeStep].SetFailed();
    }

    private static string FormatElapsed(TimeSpan t) =>
        t.TotalHours >= 1 ? t.ToString(@"h\:mm\:ss") : $"{(int)t.TotalMinutes}:{t.Seconds:00}";
}
```

`src/NexusForge/ViewModels/LogLevelToColorConverter.cs`:

```cs
using System.Globalization;
using Avalonia.Data.Converters;
using Avalonia.Media;
using Avalonia.Media.Immutable;

namespace Sylnar.ViewModels;

public class LogLevelToColorConverter : IValueConverter
{
    public static readonly LogLevelToColorConverter Instance = new();

    private static readonly IBrush Info  = new ImmutableSolidColorBrush(Color.Parse("#6CA8FF"));
    private static readonly IBrush Warn  = new ImmutableSolidColorBrush(Color.Parse("#F2B84B"));
    private static readonly IBrush Error = new ImmutableSolidColorBrush(Color.Parse("#F2685C"));
    private static readonly IBrush Debug = new ImmutableSolidColorBrush(Color.Parse("#5E6A7A"));
    private static readonly IBrush Other = new ImmutableSolidColorBrush(Color.Parse("#9AA5B4"));

    public object Convert(object? value, Type targetType, object? parameter, CultureInfo culture)
    {
        return value switch
        {
            "INFO"  => Info,
            "WARN"  => Warn,
            "ERROR" => Error,
            "DEBUG" => Debug,
            _       => Other
        };
    }

    public object ConvertBack(object? value, Type targetType, object? parameter, CultureInfo culture)
    {
        throw new NotSupportedException();
    }
}
```

`src/NexusForge/ViewModels/MainViewModel.cs`:

```cs
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using System.Windows.Input;
using Sylnar.Helpers;
using Sylnar.Models;
using Sylnar.Services;

namespace Sylnar.ViewModels;

public class MainViewModel : BaseViewModel
{
    // Sidebar order: Board & Flash, Drivers, DMA Test, BAR Probe.
    public const int BoardPage = 0, DriversPage = 1, DmaPage = 2, ProbePage = 3;

    // Log filter order in the log dock: All, Warnings, Errors.
    private const int FilterAll = 0, FilterWarnings = 1, FilterErrors = 2;

    private const int MaxLogEntries = 1000;

    private readonly LogService _logService;
    private readonly AppSettings _settings;
    private readonly BoardInfoViewModel _boardInfo;
    private readonly FlashViewModel _flash;
    private readonly DriverViewModel _driver;
    private readonly DmaTestViewModel _dmaTest;
    private readonly BarProbeViewModel _barProbe;

    private int _selectedPageIndex = BoardPage;
    private int _logFilterIndex = FilterAll;
    private int _warningCount;
    private int _errorCount;
    private bool _isLogExpanded = true;
    private string _updateStatusText = "Checking updates…";
    private bool _started;

    public BoardInfoViewModel BoardInfo => _boardInfo;
    public FlashViewModel Flash => _flash;
    public DriverViewModel Driver => _driver;
    public DmaTestViewModel DmaTest => _dmaTest;
    public BarProbeViewModel BarProbe => _barProbe;

    // ── Navigation ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public int SelectedPageIndex
    {
        get => _selectedPageIndex;
        set
        {
            // The sidebar list can report -1 while it is being rebuilt; keep a page selected.
            if (value < BoardPage || value > ProbePage) return;
            if (!SetProperty(ref _selectedPageIndex, value)) return;

            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsBoardPage));
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsDriversPage));
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsDmaPage));
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsProbePage));

            if (value == ProbePage)
                _barProbe.OnShown();
        }
    }

    public bool IsBoardPage => _selectedPageIndex == BoardPage;
    public bool IsDriversPage => _selectedPageIndex == DriversPage;
    public bool IsDmaPage => _selectedPageIndex == DmaPage;
    public bool IsProbePage => _selectedPageIndex == ProbePage;

    // ── Sidebar footer ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public string UpdateStatusText
    {
        get => _updateStatusText;
        private set => SetProperty(ref _updateStatusText, value);
    }

    public bool IsElevated { get; } = Elevation.IsAdministrator();

    public string PrivilegeText => IsElevated ? "Admin" : "Not admin";

    public Tone PrivilegeTone => IsElevated ? Tone.Neutral : Tone.Warn;

    // ── Log dock ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    /// <summary>Everything logged this session (last <see cref="MaxLogEntries"/>).</summary>
    public ObservableCollection<LogEntry> LogEntries { get; } = new();

    /// <summary>The entries that pass the current filter; this is what the dock shows.</summary>
    public ObservableCollection<LogEntry> VisibleLogEntries { get; } = new();

    public int LogCount => LogEntries.Count;

    public int LogFilterIndex
    {
        get => _logFilterIndex;
        set
        {
            if (value < FilterAll || value > FilterErrors) return;
            if (SetProperty(ref _logFilterIndex, value))
                RebuildVisibleLog();
        }
    }

    public string WarningsFilterLabel => $"Warnings {_warningCount}";
    public string ErrorsFilterLabel => $"Errors {_errorCount}";

    public bool IsLogExpanded
    {
        get => _isLogExpanded;
        set => SetProperty(ref _isLogExpanded, value);
    }

    public ICommand ClearLogCommand { get; }
    public ICommand CopyLogCommand { get; }
    public ICommand ToggleLogCommand { get; }

    public MainViewModel(
        LogService logService,
        AppSettings settings,
        BoardInfoViewModel boardInfo,
        FlashViewModel flash,
        DriverViewModel driver,
        DmaTestViewModel dmaTest,
        BarProbeViewModel barProbe)
    {
        _logService = logService;
        _settings = settings;
        _boardInfo = boardInfo;
        _flash = flash;
        _driver = driver;
        _dmaTest = dmaTest;
        _barProbe = barProbe;

        ClearLogCommand = new RelayCommand(ClearLog);
        CopyLogCommand = new AsyncRelayCommand(CopyLogAsync);
        ToggleLogCommand = new RelayCommand(() => IsLogExpanded = !IsLogExpanded);

        _logService.LogAdded += OnLogAdded;
        _logService.Info($"Sylnar v{_settings.Version} started");
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Called once when the main window first appears. Checks both USB drivers
    /// in the background so the sidebar's hardware card shows real status.
    /// </summary>
    public void OnWindowOpened()
    {
        if (_started) return;
        _started = true;
        _ = _driver.CheckBothAsync();
    }

    public void SetUpdateOutcome(UpdateCheckOutcome outcome)
    {
        UpdateStatusText = outcome switch
        {
            UpdateCheckOutcome.UpToDate => "Up to date",
            UpdateCheckOutcome.UpdateAvailable => "Update available",
            UpdateCheckOutcome.Failed => "Update check failed",
            _ => "Checking updates…"
        };
    }

    private void OnLogAdded(object? sender, LogEntry entry)
    {
        Avalonia.Threading.Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
        {
            LogEntries.Add(entry);
            AdjustCounts(entry, +1);
            if (Matches(entry))
                VisibleLogEntries.Add(entry);

            if (LogEntries.Count > MaxLogEntries)
            {
                var oldest = LogEntries[0];
                LogEntries.RemoveAt(0);
                AdjustCounts(oldest, -1);
                if (VisibleLogEntries.Count > 0 && ReferenceEquals(VisibleLogEntries[0], oldest))
                    VisibleLogEntries.RemoveAt(0);
            }

            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(LogCount));
        });
    }

    private bool Matches(LogEntry entry) => _logFilterIndex switch
    {
        FilterWarnings => entry.Level == "WARN",
        FilterErrors => entry.Level == "ERROR",
        _ => true
    };

    private void AdjustCounts(LogEntry entry, int delta)
    {
        if (entry.Level == "WARN")
        {
            _warningCount += delta;
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(WarningsFilterLabel));
        }
        else if (entry.Level == "ERROR")
        {
            _errorCount += delta;
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ErrorsFilterLabel));
        }
    }

    private void RebuildVisibleLog()
    {
        VisibleLogEntries.Clear();
        foreach (var entry in LogEntries)
            if (Matches(entry))
                VisibleLogEntries.Add(entry);
    }

    private void ClearLog()
    {
        LogEntries.Clear();
        VisibleLogEntries.Clear();
        _warningCount = 0;
        _errorCount = 0;
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(LogCount));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(WarningsFilterLabel));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ErrorsFilterLabel));
    }

    /// <summary>Copies what the dock currently shows (respects the filter).</summary>
    private async Task CopyLogAsync()
    {
        try
        {
            var sb = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
            foreach (var entry in VisibleLogEntries)
                sb.AppendLine(entry.Formatted);

            var text = sb.ToString();
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text)) return;

            var desktop = Avalonia.Application.Current?.ApplicationLifetime
                as Avalonia.Controls.ApplicationLifetimes.IClassicDesktopStyleApplicationLifetime;
            var topLevel = Avalonia.Controls.TopLevel.GetTopLevel(desktop?.MainWindow);
            if (topLevel?.Clipboard != null)
            {
                await topLevel.Clipboard.SetTextAsync(text);
                _logService.Info($"Copied {VisibleLogEntries.Count} log entries to clipboard.");
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _logService.Error($"Failed to copy log: {ex.Message}");
        }
    }
}
```

```cs
/// <summary>
/// Status colour family. View models pick a tone; the view turns it into
```

```axaml

                     ToolTip.Tip="{Binding CableType}" />

                     ToolTip.Tip="BSCAN SPI bridge bitstream used to reach the flash chip" />
        </StackPanel>
```

`src/NexusForge/Views/Controls/FlashPanel.axaml.cs`:

```cs
using Avalonia.Controls;
using Avalonia.Input;
using Avalonia.Platform.Storage;
using Sylnar.ViewModels;

namespace Sylnar.Views.Controls;

public partial class FlashPanel : UserControl
{
    public FlashPanel()
    {
        InitializeComponent();

        // Drop a firmware file anywhere on the card; validation reports bad types.
        Card.AddHandler(DragDrop.DragOverEvent, OnDragOver);
        Card.AddHandler(DragDrop.DragLeaveEvent, OnDragLeave);
        Card.AddHandler(DragDrop.DropEvent, OnDrop);
    }

    private static string? FirstLocalPath(DragEventArgs e) =>
        e.Data.GetFiles()?.Select(f => f.TryGetLocalPath()).FirstOrDefault(p => p != null);

    private void OnDragOver(object? sender, DragEventArgs e)
    {
        bool accept = DataContext is FlashViewModel { IsFlashing: false } && FirstLocalPath(e) != null;
        e.DragEffects = accept ? DragDropEffects.Copy : DragDropEffects.None;
        DropZone.Classes.Set("drag-over", accept);
        e.Handled = true;
    }

    private void OnDragLeave(object? sender, DragEventArgs e)
    {
        DropZone.Classes.Set("drag-over", false);
    }

    private void OnDrop(object? sender, DragEventArgs e)
    {
        DropZone.Classes.Set("drag-over", false);
        if (DataContext is FlashViewModel vm && FirstLocalPath(e) is { } path)
            vm.LoadDroppedFile(path);
        e.Handled = true;
    }
}

```

`src/NexusForge/Views/Controls/LogDock.axaml.cs`:

```cs
using System.Collections.Specialized;
using Avalonia.Controls;
using Avalonia.Threading;
using Avalonia.VisualTree;
using Sylnar.ViewModels;

namespace Sylnar.Views.Controls;

public partial class LogDock : UserControl
{
    private MainViewModel? _subscribedVm;

    public LogDock()
    {
        InitializeComponent();
    }

    protected override void OnDataContextChanged(EventArgs e)
    {
        base.OnDataContextChanged(e);

        if (_subscribedVm != null)
            _subscribedVm.VisibleLogEntries.CollectionChanged -= OnLogEntriesChanged;

        _subscribedVm = DataContext as MainViewModel;
        if (_subscribedVm != null)
            _subscribedVm.VisibleLogEntries.CollectionChanged += OnLogEntriesChanged;
    }

    // Keep the newest line in view.
    private void OnLogEntriesChanged(object? sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
    {
        if (e.Action != NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Add) return;

        Dispatcher.UIThread.Post(() =>
        {
            var scrollViewer = LogList.GetVisualDescendants().OfType<ScrollViewer>().FirstOrDefault();
            if (scrollViewer != null)
                scrollViewer.ScrollToEnd();
            else if (LogList.ItemCount > 0)
                LogList.ScrollIntoView(LogList.ItemCount - 1);
        }, DispatcherPriority.Render);
    }
}
```

`src/NexusForge/Views/Controls/StrokeIcon.cs`:

```cs
using Avalonia;
using Avalonia.Controls;
using Avalonia.Controls.Documents;
using Avalonia.Media;

namespace Sylnar.Views.Controls;

/// <summary>
/// A 24×24 line icon (see the Icon* geometries in Styles/Theme.axaml), stroked
/// with the inherited Foreground so it follows hover and disabled colours of
/// the button or list item it sits in.
/// </summary>
public sealed class StrokeIcon : Control
{
    public static readonly StyledProperty<Geometry?> DataProperty =
        AvaloniaProperty.Register<StrokeIcon, Geometry?>(nameof(Data));

    public static readonly StyledProperty<IBrush?> ForegroundProperty =
        TextElement.ForegroundProperty.AddOwner<StrokeIcon>();

    /// <summary>Stroke width in the icon's 24-unit space (scales with the icon).</summary>
    public static readonly StyledProperty<double> StrokeThicknessProperty =
        AvaloniaProperty.Register<StrokeIcon, double>(nameof(StrokeThickness), 1.8);

    /// <summary>Fill the shape instead of stroking it (the play triangle).</summary>
    public static readonly StyledProperty<bool> IsFilledProperty =
        AvaloniaProperty.Register<StrokeIcon, bool>(nameof(IsFilled));

    static StrokeIcon()
    {
        AffectsRender<StrokeIcon>(DataProperty, ForegroundProperty, StrokeThicknessProperty, IsFilledProperty);
        WidthProperty.OverrideDefaultValue<StrokeIcon>(16);
        HeightProperty.OverrideDefaultValue<StrokeIcon>(16);
        VerticalAlignmentProperty.OverrideDefaultValue<StrokeIcon>(Avalonia.Layout.VerticalAlignment.Center);
    }

    public Geometry? Data
    {
        get => GetValue(DataProperty);
        set => SetValue(DataProperty, value);
    }

    public IBrush? Foreground
    {
        get => GetValue(ForegroundProperty);
        set => SetValue(ForegroundProperty, value);
    }

    public double StrokeThickness
    {
        get => GetValue(StrokeThicknessProperty);
        set => SetValue(StrokeThicknessProperty, value);
    }

    public bool IsFilled
    {
        get => GetValue(IsFilledProperty);
        set => SetValue(IsFilledProperty, value);
    }

    public override void Render(DrawingContext context)
    {
        if (Data is not { } data || Foreground is not { } brush)
            return;

        var size = Math.Min(Bounds.Width, Bounds.Height);
        if (size <= 0)
            return;

        var scale = size / 24.0;
        var offset = Matrix.CreateTranslation((Bounds.Width - size) / 2, (Bounds.Height - size) / 2);
        using (context.PushTransform(Matrix.CreateScale(scale, scale) * offset))
        {
            if (IsFilled)
                context.DrawGeometry(brush, null, data);
            else
                context.DrawGeometry(null, new Pen(brush, StrokeThickness,
                    lineCap: PenLineCap.Round, lineJoin: PenLineJoin.Round), data);
        }
    }
}

```

`src/NexusForge/Views/Controls/ToneClass.cs`:

```cs
using Avalonia;
using Sylnar.ViewModels;

namespace Sylnar.Views.Controls;

/// <summary>
/// <c>ctrl:ToneClass.Tone="{Binding SomeTone}"</c> mirrors a <see cref="Tone"/>
/// into the element's classes (ok / warn / danger / info; none for neutral),
/// so Styles/Theme.axaml can colour pills, dots, notes and cards.
/// </summary>
public static class ToneClass
{
    public static readonly AttachedProperty<Tone> ToneProperty =
        AvaloniaProperty.RegisterAttached<StyledElement, Tone>("Tone", typeof(ToneClass));

    static ToneClass()
    {
        ToneProperty.Changed.AddClassHandler<StyledElement>(
            (element, e) => Apply(element, e.GetNewValue<Tone>()));
    }

    public static Tone GetTone(StyledElement element) => element.GetValue(ToneProperty);

    public static void SetTone(StyledElement element, Tone value) => element.SetValue(ToneProperty, value);

    private static void Apply(StyledElement element, Tone tone)
    {
        element.Classes.Set("ok", tone == Tone.Ok);
        element.Classes.Set("warn", tone == Tone.Warn);
        element.Classes.Set("danger", tone == Tone.Danger);
        element.Classes.Set("info", tone == Tone.Info);
    }
}
```
